# Review — 008-security-rules

## Summary

Review of 008's reopening over `d1853444..d348a641` (base-source `reopen`), 100 scope entries, 98 examined: 99 when the passes ran, read in six read-only agent slices divided by behavior — OAuth and authentication; URL carriers, configuration, telemetry and errors; dependencies, input handling and headers; release, bootstrap and pipeline documents; the rule format and 008's own artifacts; the scenarios a groom commit naming no spec (`d6d0affd`) added to other specs — and the four shared CI-pin and lockfile paths (`.github/workflows/runtime-currency.yml`, `runtime-release.yml`, `runtime.yml`, `runtime/Cargo.lock`), which this session read by their window diff. Not counted: `specs/017-derive-dont-ask/data-model.md`, which joined the scope as this run's uncommitted fix-round edit, its `CFG-ENV-005` entry read and the rest of the file left to 017's own review, which is owed; and `.ductus/reference/analyze.md`, the mirror `scripts/gen-claude-commands.sh` generates from `framework/reference/analyze.md`, which a slice read by its hunks; it is identical after the generator's substitution, and `generators.yml` reported it in sync at `d348a641`. Rule files were read whole at `HEAD`, `framework/rules/security-backend.md` and `security-frontend.md` split across three slices by rule category; each scenario and data model in scope whole, every concrete claim checked; `specs/008-security-rules/tasks.md` (280 KB) by its tasks 19 to 44, each slice the sections and the task 30, 43 and 44 items on its own rules; every other file by the hunks of the commits that brought it into scope. Deleted since the base: `framework/migrations/legacy-constitutions-region.md`, removed by `0f31a124` (071, 048), not 008's change. Two SHOULD violations, fixed in the run: dead pointers to `framework/commands/analyze.md` sections that 071 moved to `framework/reference/analyze.md`, in 068's and 069's `spec.md` (AGENTS.md, no dead references) and 008's `plan.md` (AGENTS.md, a behavior change needs a prose-claim sweep). 75 observations: 56 new, 26 fixed by the rule and 30 waived by the rule with their reasons; two of the fixes were contract breaks of 008's own artifacts, `BE-AUTHN-008`'s redirect case moved into its Statement as scenario `rules-are-as-short-as-their-required-content` requires, and `FE-CSP-008` yielding a missing `X-Frame-Options` to `BE-API-012` where `security-backend.md` is loaded, as the data model's Verification phrasing item 5 requires (owner's delegation, 2026-11-08). 19 matched stored decisions, 18 waived and one deferred to 020's scenario `every-loaded-rule-file-has-a-pass`; 4 expired, their issues fixed in the window. Two fix rounds from `start-fix-rounds`' base: round 1, re-read by a fresh agent over 23 paths with 2 exempt, found one SHOULD, the credential-header scenario's Behavior still scoping the rule to a header its library does not treat as sensitive, and two NOTEs, 070's open question and 017's data-model entry left behind by the round's `FE-VITALS-001` and `CFG-ENV-005` fixes; round 2 applied the three prescribed edits, diff-checked as matched, and the round after it was empty, so no budget stop. External sources with no fetched copy at hand were not re-checked; four waived observations name them. Not blocking.

*None.*

*None.*

*None.*

*None.*

*None.*

## Observations

- security: BE-AUTHN-012 yielded a public client's refresh-token protection to SEC-AUTHN-003 for the refresh tokens 'a server' issues, which SEC-AUTHN-003's trigger, an authorization server's, does not reach, so a non-OAuth token server's unrotated refresh token was reported by neither rule — `framework/rules/security-backend.md` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- leak: CFG-ENV-005 required an out-of-range error to name the offending value with no redaction for a sensitive setting, against CFG-ENV-006's MUST, where CFG-ENV-008 already redacts per CFG-ENV-006 — `framework/rules/configuration-cross.md` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- security: BE-API-001 does not cover Permissions-Policy or Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy, leaving them to FE-CSP-007 and FE-CSP-009, so a backend-only project serving HTML is asked for neither — `framework/rules/security-backend.md` — **waived**: Not mechanical: a single-surface coverage gap of the kind task 40 decided rule by rule, and adding the headers to a backend rule is a new obligation. (decided by the rule)
- security: BE-INPUT-016's MAY exempts only the lookup of an operator proxy's host by name, so a connection to a proxy given as an internal IP literal is exempted by no sentence of the Statement — `framework/rules/security-backend.md` — **waived**: Low confidence, and the MAY makes the operator's configured proxy the fetch's trust boundary: an IP-literal proxy is never looked up, so there is no lookup to exempt, and the Verification sets aside a fetch through a forward proxy. (decided by the rule)
- security: 008 task 43's SSRF item leaves a cross-only command-line tool uncovered because its operator supplies the URL, while 022's fetch-archive-dns-rebinding holds this repository's runtime to BE-INPUT-007 because its URL arrives as a tool argument — `specs/008-security-rules/tasks.md` — **waived**: Task 43's item records the decision as taken (owner's delegation, 2026-11-08); an agent-supplied URL in a cross-only tool is a new case for a new decision, and this repository's runtime is held to BE-INPUT-007 because this repository loads security-backend.md. (decided by the rule)
- security: BE-API-015's handshake-URL MUST NOT also bars a CSRF token there, which the OWASP WebSocket Security Cheat Sheet suggests sending in the handshake — `framework/rules/security-backend.md` — **waived**: the owner's 2026-10-20 answer keeps every token out of the handshake URL, following the same cheat sheet's advice against tokens in URL query strings; (a)'s Origin allowlist is the handshake's cross-site defense, and a CSRF token can still be checked in the first message
- security: BE-AUTHN-018's newer-token invalidation would cancel a code issued by one method when a code by another is issued in the same recovery — `framework/rules/security-backend.md` — **waived**: a recovery that verifies the first code before issuing the second complies, since the first is already used; NIST SP 800-63B-4 sets no invalidation across codes and nothing in BE-AUTHN-018 forbids that order
- bug: FE-CSRF-003's Statement bars every state-changing GET while its Not-flagged clause sets aside an OAuth client's GET redirection endpoint, so /review, which applies Statements and no routing, reports a MUST on a compliant callback /analyze sets aside — `framework/rules/security-frontend.md` — **waived**: Not mechanical: the clause names its owner, security-cross.md SEC-AUTHN-002 (a), and how /review applies a Not-flagged clause that names an owner is what 008 task 43's item gave to 020's scenario review-reports-a-defect-under-its-owner; this is one more case of it. (decided by the rule)
- convention: 077's §Edge cases double-report and waiver lists, its §Rule set scope item 5 and the changelog's tasks 43 and 44 pair list omit BE-AUTHN-012 beside SEC-AUTHN-003 for a public client's refresh-token protection, the yield 008 task 44 added — `specs/077-security-cross-rules/spec.md` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- convention: 077's plan §Moved rules keep their text says SEC-AUTHN-002 yields an ID token's signature verification to BE-AUTHN-012 and BE-AUTHN-019, where the rule keeps the signature check and yields only the algorithm allowlist and key binding, and the key's source — `specs/077-security-cross-rules/plan.md` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)

- other: 013's the-precheck-ignores-what-the-same-run-wrote Context says in the present tense that the precheck's exit line leaves section-versus-run open and pre-0.53 adopters meet its refusal, both of which 013 task 25 removed — `specs/013-text-first-artifacts/scenarios/the-precheck-ignores-what-the-same-run-wrote.md` — **waived**: 013 is done, and correcting its scenario reopens it for a review and an analysis to change a Context paragraph whose Behavior already resolves the case. (decided by the rule)
- other: 008's first Resolved Question expresses an acknowledge tier as a SHOULD whose rationale says to record the deviation, a mechanism task 19 removed with every Rationale paragraph — `specs/008-security-rules/spec.md` — **waived**: A resolved question records the decision as taken, and 073, a draft, owns how a decided rule departure is now recorded, its open question 3 citing this one. (decided by the rule)
- other: 067 task 21's pending item says the new primitive is registered at all five sites, where docs/developing-ductus.md now lists six — `specs/067-analyze-and-audit-checks/tasks.md` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- convention: 036's a-bound-test-pins-the-stated-figure says QUAL-TEST-001 requires what it states at SHOULD, and its open question calls 008's finished shortening pass in-flight — `specs/036-quality-cross-rules/scenarios/a-bound-test-pins-the-stated-figure.md` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- other: 000 task 22's pending Done-when still names the markdown-only reference, a label 079 retired — `specs/000-slash-commands/tasks.md` — **waived**: 10302897's message records leaving a 'markdown-only reference' label as read until its line is next edited (079 AC9's Resolved Questions). (decided by the rule)
- other: the pre-signed-URL MAY covers object storage only, so another signed-URL scheme, a CDN's or an application's own HMAC-signed link, falls under BE-API-013 and FE-STORAGE-003 — `framework/rules/security-backend.md` — **waived**: the owner's 2026-10-20 answer scoped the MAY to a pre-signed URL for one object and one method; whether other signed-URL schemes fit that scope is a new decision
- other: BE-AUTHN-007's Verification asks for legitimate-recovery paths during lockout, which its Statement does not name — `framework/rules/security-backend.md` — **waived**: no flag condition fires on its absence; adding it to the MUST or removing it changes scope, and no accuracy defect requires either
- other: BE-API-001's Statement requires frame protection on all HTTP responses while its Verification table sets frame-ancestors on HTML responses only — `framework/rules/security-backend.md` — **waived**: predates this window; OWASP's HTTP Headers Cheat Sheet, which BE-API-001 cites, says X-Frame-Options gives no security on a redirect or a JSON API response, while its REST Security Cheat Sheet recommends frame-ancestors 'none' on API responses, so aligning the two decides what the rule requires
- other: 008's ticked task rows 13, 14 and 15 describe FE-STORAGE-003, FE-DEPS-004 and BE-AUTHN-022 as they read before later edits — `specs/008-security-rules/tasks.md` — **waived**: spent task history: each records what the task said when it was done, and rewriting it records a fiction
- other: CFG-ENV-005, widened to every source, does not say whether a value read from several sources is range-checked as resolved or at each source — `framework/rules/configuration-cross.md` — **waived**: the value validated at startup is the one CFG-ENV-007's resolution yields, the reading CFG-ENV-003 states for presence, and no spec here reads a ranged value from two sources
- other: 036's ticked task 2 names the manifest slot it used at the time — `specs/036-quality-cross-rules/tasks.md` — **waived**: spent task history: each records what the task said when it was done, and rewriting it records a fiction
- other: configuration-cross.md's header names only analyze as enforcing it; which review pass applies the file is open — `framework/rules/configuration-cross.md` — **deferred** to `specs/020-code-review/scenarios/every-loaded-rule-file-has-a-pass.md`
- other: FE-SEMHTML-001 and FE-ARIA-001 both forbid a div with role=button — `framework/rules/accessibility-frontend.md` — **waived**: consistent overlap of two MUSTs with no contradiction; consolidating them changes rule ownership, which 069's clarify owns
- other: FE-KBD-002 and FE-CONTRAST-001 both require 3:1 for focus indicators — `framework/rules/accessibility-frontend.md` — **waived**: the owner decided FE-KBD-002's MUST carries the 3:1 ratio (069 Resolved Questions, 2026-10-19); the overlap is consistent
- other: accessibility-frontend.md's first header paragraph is long — `framework/rules/accessibility-frontend.md` — **waived**: style only; the content is accurate, and 069's first open question owns what the header claims about conformance
- other: FE-STORAGE-006 confines its Cache API MUST to service-worker code — `framework/rules/security-frontend.md` — **waived**: the rule is accurate as scoped; widening a MUST to page scripts is a new requirement for the owner, not an accuracy defect
- other: BE-REPR-001's Verification names specs/system.md for the API-wide representation — `framework/rules/api-backend.md` — **waived**: the Statement's one representation across the API needs a declared one to be checkable; the Verification names where it is declared
- other: BE-AUTHN-010's factor list reads as closed — `framework/rules/security-backend.md` — **waived**: the sentence carries no obligation keyword and the Verification enforces MFA presence, the SMS-sole ban and no skip path, consistent with BE-AUTHN-021; whether push is acceptable is a substantive owner change
- other: security-backend.md's header lists its categories in a different order from its sections — `framework/rules/security-backend.md` — **waived**: the category set matches; no artifact specifies the order, and the header matches 008's data-model table order
- other: BE-COORD-001's Verification asks a plan to say whether a lock guards correctness or efficiency — `framework/rules/concurrency-backend.md` — **waived**: the Statement's MAY turns on that classification; the Verification asks for exactly the fact it needs
- other: concurrency, observability, reliability, quality and configuration section titles are sentence case — `framework/rules/concurrency-backend.md` — **waived**: 017's and 036's data models record their files' category names in sentence case (017 task 38, an owner decision); 008's title-case bullet describes the security files
- other: 008's first resolved question reasons from CI gating — `specs/008-security-rules/spec.md` — **waived**: a resolved question records the reasoning at the time; rewriting it records a fiction
- other: fix round 1's cut of FE-VITALS-001's hedge left 070's open question saying the rule encourages stricter project-specific budgets — `specs/070-performance-frontend-rules/spec.md` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- other: fix round 1's redaction clause on CFG-ENV-005 left 017's data-model catalog entry for it listing the plain value, where its CFG-ENV-008 entry carries the clause — `specs/017-derive-dont-ask/data-model.md` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
